        xor     esi, esi
        cmp     rcx, 32
        jb      .LBB120_5
        vpxor   xmm0, xmm0, xmm0
        mov     rax, rcx
.LBB120_2:
        vpcmpeqb        ymm1, ymm0, ymmword ptr [rdx + rsi]
        vpmovmskb       edi, ymm1
        test    edi, edi
        jne     .LBB120_12
        add     rsi, 32
        add     rax, -32
        cmp     rax, 31
        ja      .LBB120_2
        test    rax, rax
        jne     .LBB120_6
        jmp     .LBB120_9
.LBB120_5:

        test    rax, rax
